  goal "fewer aches and more energy" and its check-in line "A step towards more energy."

**For the doctor (MBBS gynaecologist):**
1. Is the course's promise right: flushes "less of a problem, even when they still came", and NICE recommending CBT?
2. Are the calmer thoughts in chapter h2 fine, "Most people don’t notice" above all?
3. Chapter h4: night sweats with a fever, a cough for more than 2 weeks or weight loss mean a doctor this week and the free TB test.
   Is that the right list?
4. The sleep rules (one get-up time, the 20-minute rule, naps of about 20 minutes before 3 pm, tea before 2 pm, 15 minutes of morning
   light) and "when sleep needs a doctor" (loud snoring with pauses, very sleepy in the day, 3 months of bad sleep; CBT-I before
   sleeping pills): right for Indian women in midlife?
5. The week-4 lines on hormone therapy, for the hot-flush goal and for the others.
6. The warning signs for a woman on hormone medicine (one painful swollen calf, sudden breathlessness, weakness on one side or trouble
   speaking): the right signs, and right for the pill and a hormonal IUD as well as HRT?
7. "Often Hb and thyroid" for aches and tiredness: add vitamin D, or leave it to her doctor?
8. Chest pain, fainting or getting very breathless when she walks: an easy walk and Gentle start until her doctor says, with 112 for
   chest pain now. Is the line right, and should a yes hold the plan instead?
9. Periods stopped before 40: no plan until she taps "My doctor has seen me". Is that the right gate?
10. The checks after 40 named on the week-12 card: BP, sugar, and breast and cervical screening.

**For a clinical psychologist (RCI registered):** the Cool and calm chapters (h1 to h4), the calm chapters (q1 to q4), their crisis
line ("Thoughts of harming yourself? Call Tele-MANAS on 14416: free, any time. In danger right now, call 112.") and "Low mood or worry
on most days for 2 weeks, or it stops you doing your day: talk to your doctor. Talking therapy and medicines help." The weekly 0 to
10: is "get in your way" the right question, and should the results show it?

**For a physiotherapist:** Steady on your feet (its 3 moves and their times), the lifting chapter (s3), "Always keep a hand near a wall
or a chair", bone-safe as the rule for every midlife woman (no jumps in version 1), and Gentle start in place of Strength at home after
chest pain when she walks. The 3D loops are the nearest poses (march, walk, calf) until the new ones are made.

### 19.52 Version 39.52: pregnancy yoga by trimester (MODULE_API section 95)

Yoga for each three months of a pregnancy, in My pregnancy on 2 days a week in place of the walk, on the green tier only. The
moves Sakhi already had keep their reviewed words (Loosen up, wall push-ups, Bhramari, the chair forward bend, Shavasana). New
lines, waiting for the doctor and a physiotherapist (English, Hindi):

- Its name: "Pregnancy yoga" ("प्रेग्नेंसी योग"); on a shared phone, "Yoga". Under it: "First three months: gentle stretches and
  slow breathing" ("पहले तीन महीने: हल्के खिंचाव और धीमी साँस"), "Middle three months: stretches, strength and rest on your side"
  ("बीच के तीन महीने: खिंचाव, ताक़त और करवट लेकर आराम"), "Last three months: easy moves, long breaths out and rest on your side"
  ("आख़िरी तीन महीने: आसान हरकतें, लंबी साँस छोड़ना और करवट लेकर आराम").
- Why it helps: "Gentle yoga in pregnancy can ease aches and help you sleep and feel calmer." ("प्रेग्नेंसी में हल्का योग दर्द-तकलीफ़
  कम कर सकता है, और नींद व मन को शांत रखने में मदद करता है।") Its guide is the reviewed pregnancy dos and don'ts.
- Before the first move: "Go gently in a cool room, with water nearby, and skip any move that doesn't feel right. You should be
  able to talk while you move." ("ठंडे कमरे में, पास में पानी रखकर, आराम से करें, और जो हरकत ठीक न लगे उसे छोड़ दें। करते समय आप
  बात कर पाएँ, इतना ही ज़ोर लगाएँ।")
- The stop card, above Start ("Stop and rest if you feel", reviewed):
  - for today: "Dizziness, a headache, breathlessness before you start, or a calf that hurts or swells. Stop, rest, and tell your
    doctor today." ("चक्कर, सिरदर्द, शुरू करने से पहले ही साँस फूलना, या पिंडली में दर्द या सूजन। रुकें, आराम करें, और आज ही अपनी
    डॉक्टर को बताएँ।")
  - under "Call 112 now": "Chest pain, fainting, or regular painful tightenings in your tummy." ("छाती में दर्द, बेहोशी, या पेट में
    बार-बार दर्द वाला कसाव।"), then Help's own "In pregnancy: …" line, as it is, and Call 112.
- "Feeling unwell?" ("तबीयत ठीक नहीं?") on every move's picture opens "Stop now and rest" ("अभी रुकें और आराम करें") with the same
  lines and Call 112, 108 and 102, and two buttons: "I feel fine now" ("अब मैं ठीक हूँ") and "Stop for today" ("आज के लिए बस").
  After Stop for today: "Rest now" ("अब आराम करें"), "Your walk and yoga wait until you check your answers again; Sakhi asks you
  tomorrow. If you feel worse, don't wait: call 112." ("जवाब दोबारा देखने तक टहलना और योग रुके रहेंगे; सखी कल पूछेगी। तबीयत और
  बिगड़े, तो इंतज़ार न करें: 112 पर कॉल करें।")
- After "Hard": "Next time, go more slowly and skip any move that feels hard. If something felt wrong, tell your doctor."
  ("अगली बार और धीरे करें, और जो हरकत मुश्किल लगे उसे छोड़ दें। कुछ ठीक न लगा हो, तो अपनी डॉक्टर को बताएँ।")
- When it isn't open, on Moves and calm (UI words): "Yoga for your pregnancy comes with My pregnancy plan, after two safety
  questions."; on the first pregnancy plan: "Yoga for your pregnancy comes with the newer My pregnancy plan. To switch, tap
  "Change what I work on" in Plan settings. What you logged stays."; amber or red: "Yoga opens when your doctor says exercise is
  fine for you. Then change your answers in My safety answers."; after a sign that doesn't wait: "No yoga until you check your
  answers again." (Hindi beside each in `mods/plan.js`, `plan_py_lock_*`.)
- Her steps before she starts (UI words): the walk's line reads "Walk or move 10 minutes, or pregnancy yoga on 2 days a week"
  ("…, या हफ़्ते में 2 दिन प्रेग्नेंसी योग").
- A changed line: "Before you start"'s green tier, "Your full plan", now reads "A walk at a pace you can talk, growing over the
  weeks, with yoga for your stage of pregnancy on 2 days, the pelvic floor from week 13, and the step for your goal." ("…, और
  हफ़्ते में 2 दिन प्रेग्नेंसी के आपके महीनों का योग; …")
- The moves, each with its own words (short enough that the timer stays in view on a 360 × 800 screen; the Hindi is beside each
  in `mods/plan.js`, `PY_*` and `R.preg1` to `R.preg3`):
  - Tadasana (palm tree): "Feet a little apart and flat on the floor. Raise your arms, stretch up and breathe slowly."
  - Vrikshasana (tree) at a wall: "One hand on a wall. Rest one foot on your other ankle or calf, never on the knee. Breathe."
  - Marjariasana (cat and cow): "On hands and knees: breathe out, round your back; breathe in, lift your head. Keep it small."
    From 14 weeks the last words are "Don't let your back sag."
  - Shashankasana (child pose), first three months: "Knees wide apart, big toes together. Sit back towards your heels and stretch
    your arms forward." With knee pain, the reviewed seated forward bend.
  - Bhadrasana (butterfly): "Sit tall on a folded sheet, the soles of your feet together and your knees out. Breathe slowly." In
    the last three months: "Sit with your back against a wall, the soles of your feet together and your knees out. Breathe slowly."
  - Slow breathing (first): "Sit comfortably. Breathe in for 4 counts and out slowly for 6. Never hold your breath." Long breaths
    out (last, on a chair): "Sit tall on a chair. Breathe in for 4 counts and out slowly for 6. Never hold your breath."
  - Chair squats (middle): "Stand up from a chair slowly, using your hands if you need to, then sit down slowly. 8 times."
  - Slow sit-to-stand (last): "Feet wide apart. Stand up from a chair slowly, then sit down slowly. Use your hands if you need
    to. 6 times."
  - Side-lying leg lift (middle and last): "Lie on your side, head on your arm. Lift your top leg a little, keeping it straight,
    and lower it slowly. 8 times."
  - Rest on your side (middle and last): "Lie on your side, left or right, a pillow between your knees. Let your body go loose and
    breathe slowly."
  - Loosen up, sitting (last): "Sit tall on a chair. Roll your shoulders, then march, lifting your knees one by one."
  - Lean and sway (last): "Hands on a wall or a table, lean forward a little and sway your hips slowly, side to side or in
    circles."
  - Rock on hands and knees (last): "On hands and knees with your back flat, rock slowly forward and back. A folded sheet under
    your knees helps."

**For the doctor (rules, not words):**
1. Green tier only (no "told by your doctor" mark, no sign that doesn't wait); amber and red keep their walk or none. Should a
   green woman also be asked for her doctor's OK before yoga (decision 1 of the pregnancy advice recommended green alone)?
2. Yoga from the first weeks (the design waited until 14 weeks). The first three months' set is gentle and ends lying on the back
   (fine to 13 weeks, WHO 2020). Right to offer it from the start, with "skip any move that doesn't feel right"?
3. The stop signs: dizziness, a headache, breathlessness before starting and a calf that hurts or swells say "tell your doctor
   today"; chest pain, fainting and regular painful tightenings sit with Help's "Call 112 now" line (the safer default). Is each in
   the right group?
4. "Stop for today" holds her walk and yoga until she answers the safety questions again (as a sign that doesn't wait does). Too
   strict for a headache or tiredness?
5. "Why it helps": aches, sleep and calm. Is that claim fair for the trials (back pain, anxiety, sleep)?

**For a women's-health physiotherapist:** every move above and its time (each set is about 10 minutes; 10-second rests), the
three-month boundaries (14 and 28 weeks), child pose with knees wide in the first three months, cat and cow in the first and middle
three months ("don't let your back sag" from 14 weeks), the side-lying leg lift from 14 weeks, rocking on hands and knees and lean
and sway in the last three months, Bhramari in the middle three months, and the breathing counts (4 in, 6 out, never held). The
pictures were drawings with her bump from 14 weeks; since Version 39.54 they are the 3D figure (19.54).

### 19.53 Version 39.53: every guide leads to her plan (MODULE_API section 96)

UI words only:
- "Your plan for this" (इसके लिए आपका प्लान) above her running plan's name and, for My midlife plan, the part ("Weeks 1 to 4: Cool
  and calm");
- "A free plan for this" (इसके लिए एक मुफ़्त प्लान) above the name of the plan that fits her stage.

Both show on the guides, routines and topic pages listed in MODULE_API 96. No guide's own words changed. The hot-flush guide still
says "simple habits help a little, hormone therapy helps the most"; naming CBT there, as her plan does, would be new wording for
review. "Who treats what" now leads with the Qura doctor button its own line describes.

### 19.54 Version 39.54: the 3D figure in pregnancy yoga (MODULE_API section 97)

No new words: the three routines' moves, names and timings are 19.52's. What changes is the picture: the 3D woman who shows every
other routine now shows these, on her usual figure in the first three months (no bump shows yet) and with a bump in the middle
three months (moderate) and the last three (full). The drawings, with her bump, stay for phones without the 3D files.

**For the women's-health physiotherapist, with 19.52's list:** the 3D pictures show details the words leave open. Please check:
- Tree at a wall: her right hand flat on a wall at her side, a little below her shoulder; her left hand on her hip; her left foot
  on the inside of her right calf.
- Palm tree: her feet stay flat the whole time; her arms open out to her sides, meet overhead, and come down the same way.
- Cat and cow: she breathes out and rounds her back with her head dropping between her arms, then breathes in, lengthens her
  back with only a small dip and lifts her head. Her hands stay under her shoulders and her knees under her hips. In the first
  three months ("Keep it small") both are smaller.
- Child pose (first three months): knees wide, big toes together, hips back towards her heels, arms long in front, forehead
  towards the mat; seen from behind her and to one side, so her wide knees show.
- Lying on her side (from 14 weeks): on her right side, head resting on her right arm along the mat, left hand on the mat in front
  of her chest. At rest, both knees bent with a pillow between them. For the leg lift, the knee underneath is bent for balance and
  the top leg stays straight, rising about 30 degrees from where it rests and lowering slowly. (The left side is the mirror
  image.)
- Loosen up, sitting (last three months): sitting tall on a chair with her hands on her thighs, she rolls her shoulders up, back
  and down, then marches: elbows bent at her sides, one knee lifting a hand's width at a time, twice each side; then her hands go
  back to her thighs.
- Slow sit-to-stand (last three months): feet wide apart, toes turned out a little, arms long in front; she sits down slowly onto
  the chair and stands up slowly. Seen from the front and a little to one side, so her wide feet show.
- Lean and sway (last three months): at a table at kitchen-counter height (the words allow "a wall or a table"), hands flat on it,
  arms long, leaning forward about 45 degrees from her hips, knees soft. Her hips make one slow circle (to her left, back, to her
  right, forward), seen from her right and a little behind, so the side-to-side part shows.
- Rocking on hands and knees (last three months): her hips go back about a quarter of the way towards her heels, through the
  middle, then a little forward of her hands, her hands and knees staying put.
- Butterfly against a wall (last three months): sitting upright with her back on the wall, hands resting on her knees, knees
  lifting and dropping gently.
- Bhadrasana (first and middle three months): sitting tall on a folded sheet, soles together, knees out, her hands resting on
  her knees, her knees lifting and dropping a little as she breathes (not the cycle routine's forward lean to her toes).
- The chair squats, wall push-ups and Loosen up use the same moves as the other routines, on her with a bump.

### 19.55 Version 39.55: My period plan (MODULE_API section 98)

`plan_rv_doc_pms`, Help's `r2`, and Help's 112, Tele-MANAS 14416 and 181 lines.

New lines, waiting for the doctor (the days-before chapters for a psychologist too):

  cloth, a quiet place to rest); "Rest today, or walk 10 minutes"; "Relax, 5 minutes"; "Rest, lying down: yoga nidra"; "Get your Hb
  tested"; "Your calm-week plan" (an early night, a lighter day, 10 minutes outside); "Your pain diary for your doctor";
- their reasons: `plan_fb_kitready`, `plan_fb_restday`, `plan_fb_cyoga`, `plan_fb_relax` ("a few calm minutes a day can quiet pain

  ... in some studies it eased the days before a period": ACOG 2023 gives a conditional recommendation on low-quality evidence; food
  only, no tablet or dose);

- the fixed Hb line beside her Hb results (below 8: this week; breathless, dizzy or chest pain: today);
- the cards: "When to see a doctor", "Your second period on the plan", "Still hard? A doctor can treat painful and heavy periods and
  PMS. Take your record.";
- the 36 chapters in `mods/perplan.js` (English and Hindi): period pain p1 to p12 (heat safety in p2, the usual and not-usual pain in
  p3, medicines without names in p6), heavy periods h1 to h12 (the Hb test, iron food and tea, iron tablets as her doctor says, other
  causes of low Hb in h9, treatments in h10), the days before m1 to m12 (a 2-cycle diary and CBT skills; anger and the child-safety

  or 2 days": her kit now stays through her likely days); "endometriosis or adenomyosis (a doctor said)" in her answers' summary;
  My progress's "The week before your period: days it got in your way a lot", "Period days it got in your way" and "Leaked through
  clothes, changed at night or couldn’t do your day, from your check-ins."

UI words only: "My period plan", "My period plan is here", "Move me to it", "The same 3 taps for pads or cloth.", and "Your plan

your first 3 weeks: {x}", "On your period days: your period kit, and just before, get it ready", "On your period days: a rest
lying down (yoga nidra)", "Week 4: a look at your first month, and when to see a doctor", "From week {n}: {r} on 2 of your
walking days", "From week {n}, in the week before your period: {r}".

The phone test (2 Nov): "Relax, 5 minutes" is now "Relax, a few minutes" (Hindi "कुछ मिनट आराम"), as the slow breaths session is 4

### 19.56 Version 39.56: My pelvic floor plan (MODULE_API section 99)

Reviewed lines it reuses, unchanged: the after-birth plan's weekly "This week: any leaks?" and its 3 answers; the prolapse guide
(`intimate-prolapse`, doctor-verified 20 Oct: what it feels like, why, the two kinds of leaking, the treatment ladder, where to go,
see a doctor), whose "burning, fever or blood in the urine is usually an infection, not a weak pelvic floor" the safety screen and
the urge page reuse; the trainer's "Find the right muscles", its belly gap check, "See a doctor if" and its timer; Help's 112 and
Tele-MANAS 14416 lines.

New lines, waiting for the doctor and a women's health physiotherapist (decision 8: nothing ships to pilot women until both sign):
- the goal "Leaking urine or heaviness" and "Squeeze sets step by step, and when to see a doctor";
- the questions: "When do you leak?" (with a cough, sneeze, laugh or lift; with a sudden strong need, before I reach the toilet;
  both; I feel heaviness or a bulge down below; not sure) and "How much does it get in the way of your day?" (not at all, a little,
  quite a lot, a lot). They are our own words, not ICIQ-UI SF (a paid licence, decision 9): a reviewer compares them with its items
  3 and 4;
- "Before you start": the 7 signs and each line ("See a doctor today. Exercises don’t treat this."; "See a doctor this week. This can
  be repaired, and it shouldn’t wait 3 months of exercises." with "The repair is free at government hospitals."; the infection line;
  "See a doctor today. If it is very painful or bleeding, go now."; "See a doctor this week."; "Your plan starts with breathing and
  letting go instead of squeezes. See a doctor about the pain."; "Show this plan to your doctor first. Your sets stay lying down until
  your doctor or physiotherapist says more is fine.");
- **the 112 line**: "Suddenly unable to control your bladder or bowel, with numbness between your legs, weak legs or bad back pain"
  (Help's "Call 112 now if", the prolapse guide, chapter 10), for the doctor in particular;
- the levels (for the physiotherapist): lying 3 s × 8 and 5 s × 8; sitting 5 s × 10 with 5 quick squeezes and 7 s × 10 with 10;
  standing at a wall 5, 8 and 10 s × 10 with 10 quick; standing or walking; a let-go as long as each hold; quick squeezes of 1 s with
  2 s to let go; a level every 5 days of sets, one back for each 3 weeks away; a soft tone as each squeeze starts and as she lets go; 3 sets a day, 1 on a period day ("lying down if you
  like"), 1 a day after week 12, 3 to week 16 after a prolapse; the 3D stills for the positions;
- the steps and their reasons: "Your squeeze sets", "Find the right muscles" (lying down: squeeze as if to stop wind and urine, lift,
  then let go fully; at a new position, "Your sets are {position} now: find the squeeze that way first, then start"), "Get checked once by a doctor" (a doctor names the cause, not the app), "When the urge comes: stop, squeeze,
  breathe" (and from week 3, "wait a few minutes more before you go, a little longer each week"), "Breathe and let go, a few minutes", the
  5 habits (the knack; water, "about 6 to 8 glasses ... too little makes the bladder more urgent"; knees up on the toilet; "Less tea,
  coffee and cola, for 2 weeks", decision 5; lifting the safe way; one set standing at a task), and `plan_fb_pfl`, `plan_fb_pf_find`,
  `plan_fb_pf_chk`, `plan_fb_pf_urge`, `plan_fb_pf_habit`, `plan_fb_pf_relax`;
- the trainer: "These muscles hold up your bladder, womb and bowel. A few minutes a day, done the right way, helps stop leaks and eases
  heaviness. They take about 3 months to get stronger."; "It hurts" and its two lines; the knack after a set; "No squeezes for now.
  Breathe slowly into your belly and let the muscles down below soften. A doctor should check the pain.";
- the evening: "This week: any heaviness?", "Are you sure you’re squeezing the right way?" with "A Qura doctor or a physiotherapist can
  check it with you.", "Compared with when you started?";
- the cards: "See a doctor first"; "What did the doctor say?" (a prolapse, and exercises can help; I can exercise), and its toast "Keep 3 sets a day to week 16"; "Is it the right
  squeeze?" (a Qura doctor first; your own doctor or a women’s health physiotherapist in person; the government hospital’s gynae OPD
  as a quiet line); "Pain during your sets"; "Still leaking or heavy?" (a ring pessary, medicines for an urgent bladder, an operation;
  your doctor decides with you) and "Feeling low or alone because of this? Tele-MANAS 14416 is free.";
- the 16 chapters in `mods/pfplan.js` (English and Hindi): f1 the right squeeze, the let-go and the mistakes (no practising by stopping
  urine on the toilet, as the trainer says); f2 the knack; f3 longer holds and quick squeezes; f4 water and the 4-week check; f5 on the toilet
  (an Indian toilet already squats) and soft stools; f6 less pushing down (a cough over 3 weeks needs a doctor; less tea, coffee and
  cola for an urgent bladder); f7 standing sets; f8 lifting the safe way and halfway; f9 sets in her day; f10 when to see a doctor
  (the 112 line; today; soon, with bleeding after menopause within about 2 weeks and 2 urine infections in 6 months); f11 if
  exercises aren’t enough (a pessary, medicines, a treatment inside the vagina after menopause, an operation, all her doctor’s
  choice); f12 keep strong; after a prolapse, v1 a rest at the end of the day (10 minutes with a pillow under the hips), v2 a ring
  pessary (and when to see a doctor this week with one), v3 sex and prolapse, v4 keep strong for life. "When the urge comes" (pf-urge):
  stop, squeeze, breathe out and count to 5, walk calmly; from week 3, a little longer, "over 6 weeks your bladder learns to hold
  more".

UI words only: "My pelvic floor plan" (decision 10), "Your 2 minutes", "{n} of {m} today · level {n}, {position}", "Level {n} of
8", "Next level after {n} more days of sets", "The top level. Keep going: this keeps you strong.", "Welcome back. One level back, to
build up again.", "Start a set", "Quick squeeze", "Let go", "When did it happen?", "Not checked yet", "My doctor said more is fine",
"A doctor checked it: back to squeezes", the reveal's "What happens when" rows, the results' headings, the tiles ("Squeeze sets",
"Chapters", "Leaks and heaviness"), and the rings: "Your first set today. More after lunch and before bed." and "Your last set, then
how did today go? 1 minute".

The test pass (3 Nov), new lines for the doctor: the evening's tips for this plan ("A set takes a few minutes. Do one while the tea
boils, or in bed before you sleep."; "On a tired day, do your sets lying down. They still count."; "If a squeeze hurts, stop and tap
“It hurts” in your sets. A doctor should check the pain."; "No one can see a squeeze: do a set sitting with your family, or at a
task."; "Your sets need no space: lying down, sitting or standing, at home."); "See a doctor first if any of these is true for you."
(under "Before you start", her sets joined to another plan, with the 7 signs); "If a doctor finds a prolapse: 3 sets a day to week 16,
then keep strong." UI words: "your {t} first set", "Days 1 to 3: {x}", "In your first weeks: {x}", "Every day: your squeeze sets, 3 a
day", "None of these", and the trainer's tile "Guided squeeze-and-relax timer, step by step" (was "…, belly gap check").

Decision 6: the count is tones, vibration and big numbers. Dr. Megha's voice as an option is not built: it needs new clips from the
paid voice service (`web/tools/voice`), which waits for Mohit. Decision
7's new 3D poses (the knack scenes, side-lying) wait for the physiotherapist.

### 19.57 Version 39.57: the all-plans walk's gaps (MODULE_API section 100)

Mohit, 3 Nov: "fix all 11", after the walk in `docs/plans-walkthrough-2026-11-03.md`.
